namespace = br
# 事件ID区间：100-199, 250。新增或修改事件ID不得超出此区间喵
# 大逃杀扩圈初始化主链：br.100按顺序触发br.101到br.199，最后触发br.250收尾喵
country_event = {
	id = br.100
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		is_ai = no
		NOT = {
			has_global_flag = br_initialized
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_flag = br_initialized
		random_rim_system = {
			save_global_event_target_as = battle_royale_center
			set_star_flag = br_ring_1
			set_name = {
				key = br_ring_name_1
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = br_reached_1
